### CON-HTTP-007 — Pre-checks run before any Request is built [DRAFT — pending decision]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | wave-3 |
| Status | planned |
| Priority | medium |
| Tags | - |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | draft |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall run the pre-checks (`OPTIONS *`, raw path not starting with `/`, path validator) before any `Request` is built, so later middleware and `onError` may assume a non-null `Method` and a decoded path.

#### Rationale

Durable rule of SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-3-3 (dispatcher, automatic responses, internal RestApp), moved to the SRS at SDS close.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: For `OPTIONS *`, a raw path not starting with `/` and a raw path the path validator rejects, the dispatcher answers without constructing a `Request` and no handler, middleware or error handler is called.

### CON-HTTP-008 — A response commits status and headers at most once [DRAFT — pending decision]

| Field | Value |

| Type | constraint |
| Target | wave-3 |

| Priority | medium |

| GitHub Issue | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall commit a response's status and headers to the exchange at most once, because every adapter relies on a single commit call per exchange.

#### Rationale

Durable rule of SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-3-5 (response writers and state machine), moved to the SRS at SDS close.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: For every response path (body writers, `end`, `stream`, automatic answers and failure answers), the exchange receives at most one call that starts the response (`commit`, `sendFile`, `upgrade` or `eventStream`).

### FR-HTTP-021 — Dispatcher pipeline and pre-request checks before route lookup (wave 4)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, dispatcher |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall provide a dispatcher that takes each exchange from an `HttpAdapter` that passed the lifecycle gate of FR-APP-009 and FR-APP-013, checks the asterisk-form `OPTIONS *` first, then rejects a raw path not starting with `/` with `400`, then rejects a raw path refused by the wave 2 path validator with `400`, and only then calls the router lookup and either runs the middleware pipeline and the matched `RouteHandler` with a `Request` and a `Response` or writes the automatic response, where the pre-request rejections are answered before any `Request` is built, so no middleware, handler or `onError` sees them, and the two `400` rejections carry the standard error body.

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Through the harness, a `GET /users/7` request with `app.get("/users/:id", h)` registered reaches `h` once with a `Request` and a `Response`, and the status, headers and body `h` wrote come back through the in-memory adapter. [D-394; D-397]
- [x] AC-2: With a recording handler registered through `app.route(Method.any(), "/", h)` and `app.route(Method.any(), "/:x", h)`, `OPTIONS *` is answered `204` before path validation (so `*` is not rejected with `400`), and a validator rejection happens before the router lookup: `GET /%2e%2e` and `GET /a%2Fb` (both rejected by the `PathValidator`) are answered `400`, no lookup exception escapes, and `h` registered on the matching pattern `/:x` records no call. [wave decision: Order before lookup; D-442; D-052] Decision: the dispatcher checks in this order: `OPTIONS` with raw path `*` gives `204`, any raw path not starting with `/` gives `400`, a `PathValidator` rejection gives `400`, then the router lookup runs. This order applies to requests that passed the lifecycle gate; the unrecognised-method-token `501` comes after the validator check and before a `Request` is built.
- [x] AC-3: A raw path that the wave 2 `PathValidator` rejects is answered `400` with the standard error body of FR-APP-006 (`code` `BAD_REQUEST`, `Content-Type: application/json; charset=UTF-8`) without a router lookup, and the validator sees the raw form with a single decoding: `/%2e%2e` and `/a%2Fb` are answered `400`, while `/%252e%252e` reaches a `/:x` route with `req.param("x")` = `%2e%2e`. [wave-3 carried: path validator before lookup; D-052; wave-4 carried: standard error body; D-134] Decision: a pre-request `400` or `501` carries `Content-Type: application/json; charset=UTF-8` and the `Content-Length` of the standard body; for a `HEAD` method token the `Content-Length` is kept and no body bytes are written. [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-4-4 SDS-AC-14]
- [x] AC-4: An unrecognised method token is answered `501`, a raw path not starting with `/` `400`, a validator rejection `400` and `OPTIONS *` `204`, each before a `Request` is built, so no middleware, handler or `onError` sees them; a recording global middleware, a recording `onError` and a recording handler registered through `app.route(Method.any(), "/", h)` and `app.route(Method.any(), "/:x", h)` record that they were never called. [wave-3 residual: pre-request rejections; D-422; wave decision: Pre-request rejections]
- [x] AC-5: In every handler and middleware call `req.method()` returns a non-null `Method` and `req.path()` a non-null decoded path. [wave-3 residual: pre-request rejections; I-014]
- [x] AC-6: The HTTP-level assertions of T01, T02, T03, T05 and T06 run as tests through the in-memory adapter and the harness in `./gradlew build`. [D-397]

### FR-HTTP-022 — Automatic responses for router lookup results with the standard error body (wave 4)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, dispatcher |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall, after the global and prefix middleware ran, answer the no-match result with `404`, the method-mismatch result with `405` and an `Allow` header, the automatic-OPTIONS result with `204` and an `Allow` header, the unsupported-method result for a disabled `TRACE` or `CONNECT` (a `Method` value) with `501` and the WebSocket-only result with `426` plus `Upgrade: websocket` and `Connection: Upgrade`, where the `404`, `405`, `426` and `501` answers carry the standard error body and the `204` answers carry no body, while an unrecognised method token stays a pre-request rejection answered `501` before any `Request` is built (FR-HTTP-021).

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Through the harness, a request whose path matches no route is answered `404` with the standard error body of FR-APP-006 (`code` `NOT_FOUND`). [D-395; wave-4 carried: standard error body; D-134] Decision: the fallback answers only a response that is still `NEW`, through the request's `Response`, keeping the headers a middleware set; a response that is no longer `NEW` is left as it is. [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-4-4 SDS-AC-15]
- [x] AC-2: With only `POST /p` registered, `GET /p` is answered `405` with `Allow` listing `POST, OPTIONS`, and with `GET /r` registered `DELETE /r` is answered `405` with `Allow` including the automatic `HEAD` and `OPTIONS`, both with the standard error body (`code` `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED`). [D-395; T02; D-134] Decision: the `Allow` value joins the allowed methods (or `Method.any()` for `OPTIONS *`) with `, ` in `MethodSet` iteration (`Method` declaration) order.
- [x] AC-3: With `GET /r` and `POST /r` registered and no `OPTIONS` route, `OPTIONS /r` is answered `204` with `Allow` = `GET, HEAD, POST, OPTIONS`, no body and no `Content-Length` header. [D-395; T05; wave decision: Automatic response bodies]
- [x] AC-4: `TRACE /x` with `TRACE` disabled and `CONNECT /` with `CONNECT` disabled are each answered `501` with the standard error body (`code` `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`) after the global middleware ran, while a request with an unrecognised method token (FR-ROUTE-013) is answered `501` with the standard error body before any `Request` is built, as FR-HTTP-021 AC-4 states. [D-395; wave decision: 426 and 501; wave decision: Pre-request rejections; D-422] Decision: the dispatcher tells the two cases apart by whether the method token parses to a `Method` value.
- [x] AC-5: With a WebSocket-only route registered through the internal hook of FR-HTTP-008 AC-7, a plain `GET` to that path is answered `426` with headers `Upgrade: websocket` and `Connection: Upgrade` and the standard error body (`code` `UPGRADE_REQUIRED`), asserted through the in-memory adapter. [D-395; T06; wave decision: 426 over TinyHttpd; wave decision: 426 and 501] Decision: the upgrade flag passed to the lookup is `Router.isWebSocketUpgrade(token, Connection, Upgrade)` with each header's values joined by `, `.
- [x] AC-6: With the same WebSocket-only hook route, a `GET` carrying `Upgrade: websocket` and `Connection: Upgrade` (for which the router returns a `MATCHED` result with route kind `WEBSOCKET`) is answered with the same `426`, both headers and the standard error body, and no handshake is attempted, until wave 7 adds `app.ws`. [D-395; T06; FR-ROUTE-015]
- [x] AC-7: After `app.route(Method.any().except(Method.DELETE), "/t", h)`, `DELETE /t` through the in-memory harness is answered `405` with `Allow` = `GET, HEAD, POST, PUT, PATCH, OPTIONS` (the router's `Method` declaration order, FR-ROUTE-010 AC-1) and the standard error body. [D-395; T02]
- [x] AC-8: A recording global middleware runs once before each of the `404`, `405`, `426`, automatic `OPTIONS` and disabled-`TRACE`/`CONNECT` `501` answers above and not for the unrecognised-token `501`, and a recording `onError` records no call for any of them. [wave decision: Pre-request rejections; residual: onError scope; D-117]

### FR-HTTP-023 — Asterisk-form OPTIONS and raw paths not starting with a slash (wave 4)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, dispatcher |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall answer the asterisk-form request `OPTIONS *` (raw path `*`) with `204` and an `Allow` header listing the methods of `Method.any()`, without route lookup or path validation, and answer any other raw path that does not start with `/` with `400` and the standard error body.

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Through the in-memory adapter, `OPTIONS *` is answered `204` with `Allow` listing exactly the methods of `Method.any()` in `Method` declaration order and no body, even when no route is registered. [D-442; loop D: V1-006/V2-004] Decision: the `OPTIONS *` answer has no `Content-Length` header. [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-4-4 SDS-AC-19]
- [x] AC-2: `OPTIONS *` reaches neither the router lookup nor the path validator: with a recording handler registered through `app.route(Method.any(), "/", h)` and `app.route(Method.any(), "/:x", h)`, `OPTIONS *` is still answered `204` with the `Allow` of AC-1 (not the validator's `400` for a raw path `*`), and `h` records no call. [D-442; D-052]
- [x] AC-3: A raw path `abc` and a raw path `*` with method `GET` are each answered `400` with the standard error body of FR-APP-006 (`code` `BAD_REQUEST`) without route lookup, and the access log records one line for each. [D-442; wave-4 carried: standard error body; wave decision: Pre-request rejections]
- [x] AC-4: The `OPTIONS *` and non-`/` cases are tested through the in-memory adapter in waves 3 and 4; their TinyHttpd counterparts are not asserted in this requirement. [I-021; wave decision: Adapter halves]

### FR-HTTP-024 — Handler failure and unfinished-response handling through the wave 4 error path

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, dispatcher |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall answer an exception thrown by a handler or middleware through the error path of FR-APP-005, which gives the standard `500` error body while the response is not committed and no `onError` responded, abort the response when the exception is thrown after commit or while streaming, and, when the chain returns with the response not started and no `next.run()`, log a developer warning through the app logger and finish the response with the status and headers set so far and an empty body without waiting for a timeout.

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: A handler that throws `RuntimeException` before writing anything is answered `500` with the standard error body of FR-APP-006 (`code` `INTERNAL_ERROR`) through the harness when no `onError` is registered. [D-127; D-132; wave-4 carried: interim failure handling replaced]
- [x] AC-2: A handler that throws a checked `IOException` after setting status `201` and header `X-A: 1` but before commit is answered `500` with the standard error body; the status and headers the handler set are dropped and the exception is not rethrown to the adapter. [D-127; D-132; D-134]
- [x] AC-3: A handler that throws after commit while the response is `STREAMING` (the only committed-but-incomplete state a handler can reach before wave 5) leaves the response `ABORTED` and the in-memory adapter reports the exchange closed abnormally instead of a `500`; a handler that throws after the response is `COMPLETED` (for example after `res.send("x")`) leaves it `COMPLETED`, because abort is a no-op there, with the sent bytes unchanged and no `500`. [D-097; D-099; D-131; wave decision: abort() after completion]
- [x] AC-4: A handler that sets `status(202)` and `header("X-A", "1")` and returns without a body write or `end()`, and a middleware that returns without responding or calling `next.run()`, each yield the status and headers set so far (`202` with `X-A: 1`, and `200` for the middleware case) with an empty body, the response is `COMPLETED`, and the app logger received one `warn` naming the method and path. [D-126] Decision: the finishing behaviour is a `warn` through the app logger followed by `end()`; with the response `STREAMING` the dispatcher runs the pending stream copy on the same adapter thread and logs no warning.

### FR-HTTP-025 — Query accessors with form-urlencoded decoding and malformed-query 400 through the error path (wave 4)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, dispatcher |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall decode the raw query string as UTF-8 `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` text with `+` read as a space, make `req.query(name)` return the first value or `null` and `req.queries(name)` every value in request order or an empty list, and answer a query with malformed percent-encoding or invalid UTF-8 with `400` and the standard error body through the error path, after the middleware ran and before the handler runs.

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: For `?a=1&a=2&b=3`, `req.query("a")` returns `1`, `req.queries("a")` returns `[1, 2]`, `req.query("c")` returns `null` and `req.queries("c")` returns an empty list. [D-076; T07]
- [x] AC-2: For `?q=a+b%20c&%ED%95%9C=%EA%B8%80`, `req.query("q")` returns `a b c` and `req.query("한")` returns `글`. [D-077; T07] Decision: a pair without `=` has the empty string as its value, empty names are kept, and names and values decode `+` as space.
- [x] AC-3: A query `?x=%zz`, a query `?x=%E0%A4` (truncated UTF-8) and a query `?x=%C3%28` (invalid UTF-8) are each answered `400` with the standard error body of FR-APP-006 (`code` `BAD_REQUEST`) and the matched handler is not called. [D-078; T07; wave-4 carried: malformed query reaches middleware and onError] Decision: a malformed `%XX` or bytes that are not valid UTF-8 (decoder `CodingErrorAction.REPORT`) make the query parse raise `HttpException` with status `400`; the dispatcher parses the query after the middleware ran and before the handler, so global and prefix middleware run, a middleware that reads `req.query(...)` receives that exception, and it reaches `onError` through the error path of FR-APP-005. The query is parsed after the route middleware ran and before the handler; a request without a matched route gets the fallback answer (`404`, `405`, ...) and its query is parsed only if a middleware reads it.

### OPS-HTTP-003 — Wave 3 decisions in docs/api with the later-wave markers kept (wave 4)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | operational |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | medium |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, docs |
| Risk | low |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall record the wave 3 decisions in `docs/api/request.md`, `docs/api/response.md`, `docs/api/routing.md`, `docs/api/app.md`, `docs/api/defaults.md` and `docs/api/express-migration.md`, removing the (미확정) markers on `rawPath()`, the `send(byte[])` default `Content-Type`, `stream()` closing, the attribute type-mismatch exception, the routing-policy setting API, the `req.method()` value during automatic `HEAD` and `express.raw()`/`express.text()`, while keeping the markers owned by issues other than #4, #5, #6 and #7, whose wave 4 decisions OPS-APP-001 records.

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: `docs/api/request.md` states that `rawPath()` returns the raw, undecoded path without the query string (available through `req.query(...)`), gives its Express counterpart as `req.path` before decoding (not `req.originalUrl`), and has no (미확정) marker on the `rawPath()` row. [D-109; wave decision: Remaining markers]
- [x] AC-2: `docs/api/request.md` states that `req.attribute(name, type)` throws `ClassCastException` for a stored value of another type, without a marker, and that a value is removed by storing a typed `null` variable because a literal `null` resolves to the read overload. [D-109; wave decision: Remaining markers; wave decision: Null attribute values]
- [x] AC-3: `docs/api/response.md` states that `send(byte[])` without a `Content-Type` uses `application/octet-stream` and that `stream(InputStream, contentType)` takes ownership and closes the stream on success, failure or abort, with an example that does not use try-with-resources, and the marker line on these two points is gone. [D-109; wave decision: stream() closing; wave decision: Remaining markers]
- [x] AC-4: `docs/api/routing.md` documents `app.routerPolicy(RouterPolicy)` with `RouterPolicy.builder()` (`mergeSlashes`, `ignoreTrailingSlash`, `caseInsensitive`, `enableTrace`, `enableConnect`) as allowed only while the app is in `NEW` (before `listen()`), like every setting in `app.md` (a call outside `NEW` is rejected with `IllegalStateException` by the state guard of FR-APP-002), replaces the sentence of section '라우팅 규칙 (wave 2 결정)' saying the policy setting API name is not decided with a pointer to `app.routerPolicy(RouterPolicy)` so that no sentence calls that name undecided, and states that `req.method()` returns `HEAD` during automatic `HEAD`; its marker set is checked by OPS-ROUTE-003 AC-3. [D-398; wave decision: Remaining markers; wave decision: req.method() during automatic HEAD; wave-3 residual: marker test of wave 2]
- [x] AC-5: `docs/api/app.md` lists `routerPolicy(RouterPolicy)` among the `RestApp` methods with a Java and a Kotlin example and adds no (미확정) marker for it. [D-398; wave decision: Remaining markers]
- [x] AC-6: `docs/api/defaults.md` names `app.routerPolicy(...)` with `enableTrace`/`enableConnect` as the explicit `TRACE`/`CONNECT` policy, and that row has no (미확정) marker. [D-398; wave decision: Remaining markers]
- [x] AC-7: `docs/api/express-migration.md` no longer maps `req.originalUrl` to `req.rawPath()`: the `rawPath()` row names `req.path` before decoding as its Express counterpart without a marker, and a kept `req.originalUrl` row points to `req.rawPath()` plus the query string read through `req.query(...)` or states that it has no direct counterpart, without a marker; the `express.raw()`/`express.text()` marker is replaced with the statement that v1 has no raw or text body reader, and the raw/text bullet is removed from the undecided list (section 'Express에 있지만 아직 정해지지 않은 기능'), so the file states once, without contradiction, that v1 has no raw or text body reader. [D-109; wave decision: Remaining markers; wave decision: Body ownership] Decision: the kept `req.originalUrl` row points to `req.rawPath()` plus the query read through `req.query(...)`. [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-3-6 SDS-AC-5]
- [x] AC-8: Markers other than those this requirement and OPS-APP-001 remove stay, including `file` disposition and `redirect` in `response.md`, proxy trust, `parts()` and `jsonTree()`/`jsonMap()` in `request.md`, and the markers of `express-migration.md` owned by issues other than #4, #5, #6 and #7; `routing.md` keeps no marker once wave 4 decides `RouteBuilder.use`, and `HttpDocsTest` is re-pointed to this requirement. [wave decision: Remaining markers; D-137]

### OPS-HTTP-004 — docs/api Java examples compile against the API of the current wave (wave 4)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | operational |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | medium |
| Tags | wave-4, feasibility:high, docs |
| Risk | low |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/NaruServe/issues/5 |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall compile, in the root test suite, every ```java block of `docs/api/*.md` whose methods and types exist at the merge of the current wave, against the `NaruServe-api` main classes, skipping only blocks on an explicit, non-stale skip list (I-008, I-009).

#### Rationale

-

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: The root `build.gradle` puts the `NaruServe-api` main classes on the root test suite's test classpath (for example `testImplementation project(':NaruServe-api')`) while keeping the wave 1 root test conventions (JUnit 4, `gradleTestKit()`, the existing `test` task dependencies and system properties). [I-009; wave decision: docs examples]
- [x] AC-2: The root test `src/test/java/com/snoworca/naruserve/docs/DocsExamplesCompileTest.java` extracts every ```java fenced block of every `docs/api/*.md` file. [I-009; wave decision: docs examples]
- [x] AC-3: The test compiles a block that declares a top-level type as its own compilation unit and places any other block in a method template that declares `throws Exception`, both with the fixed imports `com.snoworca.naruserve.*`, `com.snoworca.naruserve.routing.*`, `com.snoworca.naruserve.spi.*`, `java.util.*`, `java.util.concurrent.*` and `java.io.*`, where the template has `app` (`RestApp`), `req` (`Request`) and `res` (`Response`) as fields in scope and declares the stub handlers and stub values the docs name (such as `getUser`, `id`, `user`, `ctx`, `auth`, `myAdapter` or `myCodec`); compilation runs in process through `javax.tools.ToolProvider.getSystemJavaCompiler()` with the test runtime classpath and `--release 8`, and a compilation error or a missing system compiler fails the test. [I-009; wave decision: docs examples] Decision: a non-type block is wrapped in `class DocExampleN` with fields `app`, `req` and `res` and stub `RouteHandler` fields for the handler names the docs use, inside `void example() throws Exception`; a failure names each block's file, ordinal and diagnostics. [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-3-6 SDS-AC-7] [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-3-6 SDS-AC-8] Decision: the template also declares the stub fields `ctx` (`ApplicationContext`), `auth` (`Middleware`), `myAdapter` (`HttpAdapter`) and `myCodec` (`JsonCodec`). [SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-4-10 SDS-AC-11]
- [x] AC-4: A block is skipped only when it is on an explicit list kept in the test whose entry names the file, the block (its ordinal among the file's ```java blocks or its first line) and one reason: a call to a later-wave method (named), an illustrative copy of an existing type declaration (such as `Method` or `RouteHandler`), a type of a later wave or of a library absent from the root test classpath (named, such as Gson `@SerializedName` or `UploadFile`), or an app-defined example type the block assumes (named, such as `UserService` or `Database`). ### CON-HTTP-010 — Pre-request rejections are answered before any Request exists [DRAFT — pending decision]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | wave-4 |
| Status | planned |
| Priority | medium |
| Tags | - |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | draft |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | - |

#### Requirement

The system shall answer every pre-request rejection (unrecognised method token, raw path not starting with `/`, `PathValidator` rejection, `OPTIONS *`) and every lifecycle-gate `503` before a `Request` is built, so middleware, handlers and `onError` may assume that `req.method()` and `req.path()` are valid.

#### Rationale

Durable rule of SDS 2026-10-19.naruserve.naruserve-v1-wave-4-4 (middleware pipeline, next contract, dispatcher order), moved to the SRS at SDS close; binds FR-HTTP-021.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: A recording global middleware, a recording handler and a recording `onError` record no call for any pre-request rejection or lifecycle-gate `503`, and in every call they do receive `req.method()` returns a non-null `Method` and `req.path()` a non-null decoded path.
